([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/p-value-combination-fisher-method))

- **p-value-combination-weighted-fisher**: Use when individual metabolomics studies report only P-values and fold-changes (no raw data, SD, or variance) and a combined significance per metabolite across studies is needed. - **p-value-comparison-table-compilation**: Use when benchmarking group-level metabolite pathway enrichment tools on a shared metabolite list and you need a side-by-side comparison of each tool's top significant pathway and its enrichment P value. - **p-value-computation-and-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when after matched lipid abundances have been grouped by experimental condition and descriptive statistics (mean, SD, median) calculated per group. - **p-value-cutoff-threshold-filtering**: Use when after differential expression analysis (DEA) has produced p-values and log fold-change values for multiple omics layers (genes, miRNA, proteins, lipids), and you need to select only statistically significant features for pathway enrichment. - **p-value-distribution-aggregation**: Use when comparing robustness of pathway ranking methods on metabolomics peak data by repeating an analysis over multiple random feature-removal replicates and summarising each method's p-value distribution as mean and variance. - **p-value-distribution-normalization**: Use when integrating p-values/scores from multiple genomic assays whose distributions occupy different ranges — e.g. before SMITE scorePval — and you need scores comparable to a reference (expression) without changing within-feature ranking. - **p-value-multiple-testing-adjustment**: Use when combining two independent differential-expression p-values (sender ligand, receiver receptor) into one LR-pair significance and needing a multiple-testing-adjusted p-value to filter candidate ligand-receptor associations. - **p-value-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a pathway overrepresentation analysis produces a table of raw per-pathway p-values from Fisher's exact test (or Fisher's-method combined metabolite/gene p-values) and they must be corrected for multiple testing before ranking or cutoff filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/p-value-multiple-testing-correction))
- **p-value-rank-correlation-analysis**: Use when you need to compare per-gene p-value rankings produced by two versions or variants of a feature-scanning method across multiple datasets, to quantify whether a methodological change (e.g., binary detection vs continuous activity) preserves DEG predictions. - **p-value-significance-verification**: Use when two SVG/DEG detection methods have been run on the same spatial dataset and you must verify and report which features agree or show discrepant ranks and p-values between them. - **p-value-trace-visualization**: Use when you have run progressive permutation of a binary grouping factor over a microbiome feature table and need to visually assess whether the most differential features from the original data retain signal strength (as -log10 p-values) as mixing approaches full permutation. - **p10**: The Power of Ten: Gerard Holzmann's (NASA/JPL) ten rules for safety-critical code. They keep control flow simple, bound every loop, allocate no memory after init, keep functions short, require dense assertions, minimal scope, checked returns and parameters, a restricted preprocessor, and restricted pointers, and demand zero warnings. Use when writing, reviewing, or refactoring code that must be reliable or statically verifiable, or when the user runs /p10 or asks for the Power of Ten rules. ([cnordenb/atlas-maker](https://github.com/cnordenb/atlas-maker/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/p10))

- **p1000-authentication-failed-against-da-f75e7711899b8560**: Fixes Prisma error P1000 'Authentication failed against database server' when the database is reachable but rejects the credentials, most often because a native Postgres install already holds port 5432 while the app points at the Docker container. Use when P1000 names the right server. Not for unreachable servers (P1001) or missing databases (P1003). ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/p1000-authentication-failed-against-da-f75e7711899b8560))
- **p1001-can-t-reach-database-server-at-y-d4a56ea505d5c863**: Fixes Prisma error P1001 'Can't reach database server' by diagnosing which part of the connection path is wrong: host, port, pooler mode, or a server that is not actually running. Use when Prisma fails to open any connection at all. Not for authentication failures (P1000) or missing databases (P1003). ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/p1001-can-t-reach-database-server-at-y-d4a56ea505d5c863))
- **p1003-database-mydb-does-not-exist-on-03d1db6ed0394f57**: Fixes Prisma error P1003 'Database does not exist on the database server' by creating the missing database and then running migrations against it. Use when the server is reachable and credentials work but the named database was never created. Not for unreachable servers (P1001) or auth failures (P1000). ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/p1003-database-mydb-does-not-exist-on-03d1db6ed0394f57))
- **p1017-server-has-closed-the-connection-1f4f5f623a3d7bd0**: This skill fixes Prisma error P1017, where the database server closes the connection mid-query. Use it when Prisma throws P1017 with PgBouncer, RDS Proxy, Neon, or Supabase poolers, or after idle periods. It covers pooler timeouts, stale connections, and connection_limit tuning. Используй когда нужно прогнать идею через весь пайплайн де-рискинга, "от проблемы до MVP", запустить весь flow p2m целиком.
